# Observer Log

Append-only one-line-per-pass log written by the **Codex observer**. The **Codex executor** checks this file during each goal-loop turn for new entries and pending nudges.

Format:
```
<iso8601> <CONTINUE|NUDGE NNN|REDIRECT|STOP|ESCALATE> — <short slug>
```

Acknowledgements (written by the Codex executor):
```
<iso8601> ack <NNN>      [executor acted on nudge NNN]
<iso8601> defer <NNN>: <reason>   [executor could not act in current slice]
```
